{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.\n\nSummary:\n1. Primary Request and Intent:\n   Deploy A1 VLA-only baseline evaluation on LIBERO-Object benchmark on server bjb1-32546 with cuda:0. Specific parameters: seed=42, n_rollouts=20, benchmark=object, VLA-only mode (no CRM). The A1 model is from HuggingFace repo `spatialtemporal-ai/a1-libero` (~8.8B params). The task says \"如果代码没有 A1 支持，汇报需要什么代码修改，不要自己写复杂的集成代码\" (if no A1 support, report needed changes, don't write complex integration code). Should use `submit_training_job` for running, not nohup. Environment activation: `source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`.\n\n2. Key Technical Concepts:\n   - A1 VLA model: AffordVLA class from `a1.vla.affordvla`, based on Molmo architecture with Qwen2-7B LLM + CLIP ViT-L/14 vision backbone\n   - Flow-matching action head with 10 inference steps, action_dim=32 (truncate to 7 for LIBERO), chunk=10, open-loop=8\n   - BOUNDS_Q99 action normalization with dataset_statistics.json containing per-suite norm stats\n   - Proprioception: 7D state (eef_pos + axisangle + gripper_qpos), normalized separately, gripper gets special processing\n   - Action processing: normalize gripper [0,1]→[-1,+1], then invert sign for A1\n   - The existing project (ACR-VLA) uses SmolVLA and Pi05 wrappers, evaluate.py doesn't support A1\n   - A1's robot_experiments code imports tensorflow which is incompatible with current environment (numpy 2.2.6 vs tf requiring numpy < 2.0)\n   - Model is saved in fp32 (33.8GB), needs bf16 conversion for 24GB RTX 4090\n\n3. Files and Code Sections:\n   - `/root/acr-vla-conformal-refinement/src/evaluate.py` (server)\n     - Main eval script for ACR-VLA project. In the config.yaml, `tokenizer.identifier` is `Qwen/Qwen2-7B` and `tokenizer_dir` is `null`\n3. `build_tokenizer` calls `AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(tokenizer_type, cache_dir=cache_dir)` where `cache_dir` is `None` when `tokenizer_dir` is `None`\n\n\n4. The real issue is that I can't modify the config.yaml in the checkpoint, but I could set `HF_HOME` to point to where the tokenizer was cached, or create a symlink so that `Qwen/Qwen2-7B` resolves to the local tokenizer at `/root/autodl-tmp/models/qwen2-7b-tokenizer/`.\n\nActually, the cleanest solution is to override the tokenizer config after loading it—I'll set both `tokenizer_dir` and `identifier` to the local path in the smoke test and eval script. \"libero_90\",\n}\nUNNORM_KEY_MAP = {\n    \"object\": \"libero_object_no_noops\",\n    \"spatial\": \"libero_spatial_no_noops\",\n    \"goal\": \"libero_goal_no_noops\",\n    \"long\": \"libero_10_no_noops\",\n    \"90\": \"libero_90_no_noops\",\n}\nNUM_OPEN_LOOP_STEPS = 8\nNUM_STEPS_WAIT = 10\nNORMALIZATION_TYPE = NormalizationType.BOUNDS_Q99\n\n\ndef set_seed(seed):\n    import random\n    torch.manual_seed(seed)\n    torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(seed)\n    np.random.seed(seed)\n    random.seed(seed)\n    torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True\n    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False\n    os.environ[\"PYTHONHASHSEED\"] = str(seed)\n\n\ndef load_a1_model(checkpoint_path, device):\n    cfg_path = resource_path(Path(checkpoint_path), \"config.yaml\")\n    model_cfg = ModelConfig.load(cfg_path, key=\"model\", validate_paths=False)\n    model_cfg.num_diffusion_inference_steps = 10\n    model_cfg.llm_causal_attention = False\n\n    model = AffordVLA(model_cfg)\n    state_dict_path = resource_path(Path(checkpoint_path), \"model.pt\")\n    state_dict = torch.load(str(state_dict_path), map_location=\"cpu\")\n    model.load_state_dict(state_dict, strict=True)\n    del state_dict\n    torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n\n    model = model.to(torch.bfloat16)\n    model.to(device)\n    model.eval()\n    print(f\"[a1] Model loaded, params={model.num_params():,d}, device={device}\")\n    return model\n\n\ndef load_norm_stats(checkpoint_path):\n    stats_path = os.path.join(checkpoint_path, \"dataset_statistics.json\")\n    with open(stats_path) as f:\n        return json.load(f)\n\n\ndef normalize_proprio_bounds_q99(proprio, stats):\n    q99 = np.array(stats[\"q99\"])\n    q01 = np.array(stats[\"q01\"])\n    mask = np.array(stats.get(\"mask\", np.ones_like(q01, dtype=bool)))\n    return np.where(mask, 2.0 * (proprio - q01) / (q99 - q01 + 1e-8) - 1.0, proprio)\n\n\ndef unnormalize_actions_bounds_q99(normalized, stats):\n    q99 = np.array(stats[\"q99\"])\n    q01 = np.array(stats[\"q01\"])\n    mask = np.array(stats[\"mask\"])\n    return np.where(mask, 0.5 * (normalized + 1) * (q99 - q01) + q01, normalized)\n\n\ndef get_libero_image(obs):\n    return obs[\"agentview_image\"][::-1]\n\n\ndef get_libero_wrist_image(obs):\n    return obs[\"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\"][::-1]\n\n\ndef prepare_obs(obs, resize_size):\n    img = get_libero_image(obs)\n    wrist = get_libero_wrist_image(obs)\n    img_pil = Image.fromarray(img).resize(resize_size, Image.BILINEAR)\n    wrist_pil = Image.fromarray(wrist).resize(resize_size, Image.BILINEAR)\n    state = np.concatenate([\n        obs[\"robot0_eef_pos\"],\n        quat2axisangle(obs[\"robot0_eef_quat\"]),\n        obs[\"robot0_gripper_qpos\"],\n    ])\n    return np.array(img_pil), np.array(wrist_pil), state\n\n\n@torch.no_grad()\ndef predict_actions(model, device, img_np, wrist_np, state, task_desc,\n                    norm_stats, unnorm_key, preprocessor, sequence_length):\n    proprio = torch.tensor(state, dtype=torch.float32).unsqueeze(0)\n    gripper_raw = proprio[..., -1]\n    gripper_norm = torch.clamp(2.0 * (gripper_raw - 0.02) / (0.04 + 1e-8) - 1.0, -1.0, 1.0)\n\n    proprio_np = proprio[..., :-1].numpy()\n    proprio_stats = norm_stats[unnorm_key][\"proprio\"]\n    proprio_np = normalize_proprio_bounds_q99(proprio_np, proprio_stats)\n    proprio[..., :-1] = torch.from_numpy(proprio_np).float()\n    proprio[..., -1] = gripper_norm\n\n    proprio = proprio.unsqueeze(1).numpy()\n    pad_width = [(0, 0)] * (proprio.ndim - 1) + [(0, model.config.proprio_dim - proprio.shape[-1])]\n    proprio = np.pad(proprio, pad_width, constant_values=0)\n\n    dummy_action = np.zeros((model.config.num_actions_chunk, model.config.action_dim), dtype=np.float32)\n    input_data = {\n        \"question\": task_desc,\n        \"proprio\": proprio,\n        \"action\": dummy_action,\n        \"action_pad_mask\": np.zeros_like(dummy_action, dtype=bool),\n        \"answer\": \"Action\",\n        \"style\": \"action\",\n        \"metadata\": {},\n        \"images\": [Image.fromarray(img_np), Image.fromarray(wrist_np)],\n    }\n\n    processed = preprocessor(input_data)\n\n    collator = MMCollatorForAction(\n        model_config=model.config,\n        use_proprio=True,\n        max_sequence_length=sequence_length,\n        include_metadata=False,\n        pad=\"to_max\",\n        max_crops=model.config.get_max_crops(),\n    )\n    batch = collator([processed])\n    for k in batch:\n        if isinstance(batch[k], torch.Tensor):\n            batch[k] = batch[k].to(device)\n\n    model_inputs = {\n        \"input_ids\": batch[\"input_ids\"],\n        \"images\": batch.get(\"images\"),\n        \"image_masks\": batch.get(\"image_masks\"),\n        \"attention_mask\": batch.get(\"attention_mask\"),\n        \"attention_bias\": batch.get(\"attention_bias\"),\n        \"response_mask\": (batch[\"loss_masks\"] > 0) if \"loss_masks\" in batch else None,\n        \"image_input_idx\": batch.get(\"image_input_idx\"),\n        \"subsegment_ids\": batch.get(\"subsegment_ids\"),\n        \"position_ids\": batch.get(\"position_ids\"),\n        \"action_proprio\": batch.get(\"proprio\"),\n        \"proprio_token_idx\": batch.get(\"proprio_token_idx\"),\n        \"output_hidden_states\": False,\n        \"use_cache\": True,\n    }\n\n    norm_actions = model.predict_actions(**model_inputs)\n    norm_actions = norm_actions.to(torch.float32).cpu().numpy()\n    norm_actions = norm_actions[..., :7]\n\n    action_stats = norm_stats[unnorm_key][\"action\"]\n    actions = unnormalize_actions_bounds_q99(norm_actions, action_stats)\n\n    if actions.ndim == 3:\n        actions = actions[0]\n    return actions\n\n\ndef process_action(action):\n    a = action.copy()\n    a[-1] = 1.0 if a[-1] > 0.5 else -1.0\n    a[-1] = -a[-1]\n    return a\n\n\ndef run_rollout(env, init_state, model, device, task_desc, max_steps,\n                norm_stats, unnorm_key, preprocessor, sequence_length):\n    env.reset()\n    obs = env.set_init_state(init_state)\n\n    for _ in range(NUM_STEPS_WAIT):\n        obs, _, _, _ = env.step(np.array([0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, -1], dtype=np.float64))\n\n    resize_size = tuple(model.config.vision_backbone.image_default_input_size)\n    action_queue = deque()\n\n    for step in range(max_steps):\n        try:\n            if len(action_queue) == 0:\n                img_np, wrist_np, state = prepare_obs(obs, resize_size)\n                chunk = predict_actions(\n                    model, device, img_np, wrist_np, state, task_desc,\n                    norm_stats, unnorm_key, preprocessor, sequence_length,\n                )\n                for i in range(min(NUM_OPEN_LOOP_STEPS, len(chunk))):\n                    action_queue.append(chunk[i])\n\n            raw_action = action_queue.popleft()\n            action = process_action(raw_action)\n            obs, reward, done, info = env.step(action.tolist())\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"[rollout] step {step}: {e}\")\n            traceback.print_exc()\n            return False, step\n\n        if done or info.get(\"success\", False):\n            return True, step + 1\n\n    return False, max_steps\n\n\ndef evaluate(args):\n    set_seed(args.seed)\n    device = torch.device(f\"cuda:{args.gpu}\")\n\n    print(f\"[a1-eval] benchmark={args.benchmark}, n_rollouts={args.n_rollouts}, seed={args.seed}\")\n\n    model = load_a1_model(args.checkpoint, device)\n    norm_stats = load_norm_stats(args.checkpoint)\n    unnorm_key = UNNORM_KEY_MAP[args.benchmark]\n    assert unnorm_key in norm_stats, f\"Key {unnorm_key} not in norm_stats\"\n\n    preprocessor = build_mm_preprocessor(\n        model_config=model.config,\n        shuffle_messages=True,\n        require_image_features=True,\n        is_training=False,\n    )\n\n    max_steps = SUITE_MAX_STEPS[args.benchmark]\n    libero_suite_name = SUITE_TO_LIBERO[args.benchmark]\n\n    benchmark_dict = benchmark.get_benchmark_dict()\n    task_suite = benchmark_dict[libero_suite_name]()\n    num_tasks = task_suite.n_tasks\n\n    print(f\"[a1-eval] suite={libero_suite_name}, tasks={num_tasks}, max_steps={max_steps}\")\n\n    from libero.libero.envs import OffScreenRenderEnv\n    results = {}\n    total_success = 0\n    total_episodes = 0\n\n    for task_id in range(num_tasks):\n        task = task_suite.get_task(task_id)\n        task_name = task.name\n        task_desc = task.language\n        init_states = task_suite.get_task_init_states(task_id)\n\n        env_args = {\n            \"bddl_file_name\": task.bddl_file,\n            \"camera_heights\": 256,\n            \"camera_widths\": 256,\n            \"has_renderer\": False,\n            \"has_offscreen_renderer\": True,\n            \"use_camera_obs\": True,\n            \"camera_names\": [\"agentview\", \"robot0_eye_in_hand\"],\n        }\n        env = OffScreenRenderEnv(**env_args)\n\n        task_successes = 0\n        for trial in range(args.n_rollouts):\n            set_seed(args.seed + trial)\n            init_state = init_states[trial % len(init_states)]\n\n            success, steps = run_rollout(\n                env, init_state, model, device, task_desc, max_steps,\n                norm_stats, unnorm_key, preprocessor, args.sequence_length,\n            )\n            task_successes += int(success)\n            total_success += int(success)\n            total_episodes += 1\n\n            status = \"OK\" if success else \"FAIL\"\n            print(f\"  task {task_id}/{num_tasks} [{task_name}] \"\n                  f\"trial {trial+1}/{args.n_rollouts}: {status} ({steps} steps)\")\n\n        task_sr = task_successes / args.n_rollouts\n        results[task_name] = {\"success_rate\": task_sr, \"successes\": task_successes, \"trials\": args.n_rollouts}\n        print(f\"[a1-eval] Task {task_id} [{task_name}]: SR={task_sr:.1%} ({task_successes}/{args.n_rollouts})\")\n\n        env.close()\n\n    overall_sr = total_success / total_episodes if total_episodes > 0 else 0.0\n    print(f\"\\n[a1-eval] Overall: SR={overall_sr:.1%} ({total_success}/{total_episodes})\")\n\n    summary = {\n        \"model\": \"a1-libero\",\n        \"benchmark\": args.benchmark,\n        \"mode\": \"vla_only\",\n        \"seed\": args.seed,\n        \"n_rollouts\": args.n_rollouts,\n        \"overall_success_rate\": overall_sr,\n        \"total_successes\": total_success,\n os.environ[\"PYTHONHASHSEED\"] = str(seed)\n\n\ndef load_a1_model(checkpoint_path, device, tokenizer_path=None):\n    cfg_path = resource_path(Path(checkpoint_path), \"config.yaml\")\n    model_cfg = ModelConfig.load(cfg_path, key=\"model\", validate_paths=False)\n    model_cfg.num_diffusion_inference_steps = 10\n    model_cfg.llm_causal_attention = False\n\n    if tokenizer_path:\n        model_cfg.tokenizer.identifier = tokenizer_path\n\n    model = AffordVLA(model_cfg)\n    state_dict_path = resource_path(Path(checkpoint_path), \"model.pt\")\n    state_dict = torch.load(str(state_dict_path), map_location=\"cpu\")\n    model.load_state_dict(state_dict, strict=True)\n    del state_dict\n    torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n\n    model = model.to(torch.bfloat16)\n os.path.join(checkpoint_path, \"dataset_statistics.json\")\n    with open(stats_path) as f:\n        return json.load(f)\n\n\ndef normalize_proprio_bounds_q99(proprio, stats):\n    q99 = np.array(stats[\"q99\"])\n    q01 = np.array(stats[\"q01\"])\n    mask = np.array(stats.get(\"mask\", np.ones_like(q01, dtype=bool)))\n    return np.where(mask, 2.0 * (proprio - q01) / (q99 - q01 + 1e-8) - 1.0, proprio)\n\n\ndef unnormalize_actions_bounds_q99(normalized, stats):\n    q99 = np.array(stats[\"q99\"])\n    q01 = np.array(stats[\"q01\"])\n    mask = np.array(stats[\"mask\"])\n    return np.where(mask, 0.5 * (normalized + 1) * (q99 - q01) + q01, normalized)\n\n\ndef get_libero_image(obs):\n    return obs[\"agentview_image\"][::-1]\n\n\ndef get_libero_wrist_image(obs):\n    return obs[\"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\"][::-1]\n\n\ndef prepare_obs(obs, resize_size):\n    img = get_libero_image(obs)\n    wrist = get_libero_wrist_image(obs)\n    img_pil = Image.fromarray(img).resize(resize_size, Image.BILINEAR)\n    wrist_pil = Image.fromarray(wrist).resize(resize_size, Image.BILINEAR)\n    state = np.concatenate([\n        obs[\"robot0_eef_pos\"],\n        quat2axisangle(obs[\"robot0_eef_quat\"]),\n        obs[\"robot0_gripper_qpos\"],\n    ])\n    return np.array(img_pil), np.array(wrist_pil), state\n\n\n@torch.no_grad()\ndef predict_actions(model, device, img_np, wrist_np, state, task_desc,\n                    norm_stats, unnorm_key, preprocessor, sequence_length):\n    proprio = torch.tensor(state, dtype=torch.float32).unsqueeze(0)\n    gripper_raw = proprio[..., -1]\n    gripper_norm = torch.clamp(2.0 * (gripper_raw - 0.02) / (0.04 + 1e-8) - 1.0, -1.0, 1.0)\n\n    proprio_np = proprio[..., :-1].numpy()\n    proprio_stats = norm_stats[unnorm_key][\"proprio\"]\n    proprio_np = normalize_proprio_bounds_q99(proprio_np, proprio_stats)\n    proprio[..., :-1] = torch.from_numpy(proprio_np).float()\n    proprio[..., -1] = gripper_norm\n\n    proprio = proprio.unsqueeze(1).numpy()\n    pad_width = [(0, 0)] * (proprio.ndim - 1) + [(0, model.config.proprio_dim - proprio.shape[-1])]\n    proprio = np.pad(proprio, pad_width, constant_values=0)\n\n    dummy_action = np.zeros((model.config.num_actions_chunk, model.config.action_dim), dtype=np.float32)\n    input_data = {\n        \"question\": task_desc,\n        \"proprio\": proprio,\n        \"action\": dummy_action,\n        \"action_pad_mask\": np.zeros_like(dummy_action, dtype=bool),\n preprocessor(input_data)\n\n    collator = MMCollatorForAction(\n        model_config=model.config,\n        use_proprio=True,\n        max_sequence_length=sequence_length,\n        include_metadata=False,\n        pad=\"to_max\",\n        max_crops=model.config.get_max_crops(),\n    )\n    batch = collator([processed])\n    for k in batch:\n        if isinstance(batch[k], torch.Tensor):\n            batch[k] = batch[k].to(device)\n\n    model_inputs = {\n        \"input_ids\": batch[\"input_ids\"],\n        \"images\": batch.get(\"images\"),\n        \"image_masks\": batch.get(\"image_masks\"),\n        \"attention_mask\": batch.get(\"attention_mask\"),\n        \"attention_bias\": batch.get(\"attention_bias\"),\n        \"response_mask\": (batch[\"loss_masks\"] > batch.get(\"position_ids\"),\n        \"action_proprio\": batch.get(\"proprio\"),\n        \"proprio_token_idx\": batch.get(\"proprio_token_idx\"),\n        \"output_hidden_states\": False,\n        \"use_cache\": True,\n    }\n\n    norm_actions = model.predict_actions(**model_inputs)\n    norm_actions = norm_actions.to(torch.float32).cpu().numpy()\n    norm_actions = norm_actions[..., :7]\n\n    action_stats = norm_stats[unnorm_key][\"action\"]\n    actions = unnormalize_actions_bounds_q99(norm_actions, action_stats)\n\n    if actions.ndim == 3:\n        actions = actions[0]\n    return actions\n\n\ndef process_action(action):\n    a = action.copy()\n    a[-1] = 1.0 if a[-1] > 0.5 else -1.0\n    a[-1] = -a[-1]\n    return a\n\n\ndef run_rollout(env, init_state, model, device, task_desc, max_steps,\n                norm_stats, unnorm_key, preprocessor, sequence_length):\n    env.reset()\n    obs = env.set_init_state(init_state)\n\n    for _ in range(NUM_STEPS_WAIT):\n        obs, _, _, _ = env.step(np.array([0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, -1], dtype=np.float64))\n\n    resize_size = tuple(model.config.vision_backbone.image_default_input_size)\n    action_queue = deque()\n\n    for step in range(max_steps):\n        try:\n            if len(action_queue) == 0:\n                img_np, wrist_np, state = prepare_obs(obs, resize_size)\n                chunk = predict_actions(\n                    model, device, img_np, wrist_np, state, task_desc,\n                    norm_stats, unnorm_key, preprocessor, sequence_length,\n                )\n                for i in range(min(NUM_OPEN_LOOP_STEPS, len(chunk))):\n                    action_queue.append(chunk[i])\n\n            raw_action = action_queue.popleft()\n            action = process_action(raw_action)\n            obs, reward, done, info = env.step(action.tolist())\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"[rollout] step {step}: {e}\")\n            traceback.print_exc()\n            return False, step\n\n        if done or info.get(\"success\", False):\n            return True, step + 1\n\n    return False, max_steps\n\n\ndef evaluate(args):\n    set_seed(args.seed)\n    device = torch.device(f\"cuda:{args.gpu}\")\n\n    print(f\"[a1-eval] benchmark={args.benchmark}, n_rollouts={args.n_rollouts}, seed={args.seed}\")\n\n    model = load_a1_model(args.checkpoint, device)\n    norm_stats = load_norm_stats(args.checkpoint)\n    unnorm_key = UNNORM_KEY_MAP[args.benchmark]\n    assert unnorm_key in norm_stats, f\"Key {unnorm_key} not in norm_stats\"\n\n    preprocessor = build_mm_preprocessor(\n        model_config=model.config,\n        shuffle_messages=True,\n        require_image_features=True,\n        is_training=False,\n    )\n\n    max_steps = SUITE_MAX_STEPS[args.benchmark]\n    libero_suite_name = SUITE_TO_LIBERO[args.benchmark]\n\n    benchmark_dict = benchmark.get_benchmark_dict()\n    task_suite = benchmark_dict[libero_suite_name]()\n    num_tasks = task_suite.n_tasks\n\n    print(f\"[a1-eval] suite={libero_suite_name}, tasks={num_tasks}, max_steps={max_steps}\")\n\n    from libero.libero.envs import OffScreenRenderEnv\n    results = {}\n    total_success = 0\n    total_episodes = 0\n\n    for task_id in range(num_tasks):\n        task = task_suite.get_task(task_id)\n        task_name = task.name\n        task_desc = task.language\n        init_states = task_suite.get_task_init_states(task_id)\n\n        env_args = {\n            \"bddl_file_name\": task.bddl_file,\n            \"camera_heights\": 256,\n            \"camera_widths\": 256,\n            \"has_renderer\": False,\n            \"has_offscreen_renderer\": True,\n            \"use_camera_obs\": True,\n            \"camera_names\": [\"agentview\", \"robot0_eye_in_hand\"],\n        }\n        env = OffScreenRenderEnv(**env_args)\n\n        task_successes = 0\n        for trial in range(args.n_rollouts):\n            set_seed(args.seed + trial)\n            init_state = init_states[trial % len(init_states)]\n\n            success, steps = run_rollout(\n                env, init_state, model, device, task_desc, max_steps,\n                norm_stats, unnorm_key, preprocessor, args.sequence_length,\n            )\n            task_successes += int(success)\n            total_success += int(success)\n            total_episodes += 1\n\n            status = \"OK\" if success else \"FAIL\"\n            print(f\"  task {task_id}/{num_tasks} [{task_name}] \"\n                  f\"trial {trial+1}/{args.n_rollouts}: {status} ({steps} steps)\")\n\n        task_sr = task_successes / args.n_rollouts\n        results[task_name] = {\"success_rate\": task_sr, \"successes\": task_successes, \"trials\": args.n_rollouts}\n        print(f\"[a1-eval] Task {task_id} [{task_name}]: SR={task_sr:.1%} ({task_successes}/{args.n_rollouts})\")\n\n        env.close()\n\n    overall_sr = total_success / total_episodes if total_episodes > 0 else 0.0\n    print(f\"\\n[a1-eval] Overall: SR={overall_sr:.1%} ({total_success}/{total_episodes})\")\n\n    summary = {\n        \"model\": \"a1-libero\",\n        \"benchmark\": args.benchmark,\n out_path.write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=2))\n    print(f\"[a1-eval] Results saved to {args.output_file}\")\n    return summary\n\n\ndef main():\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n                model, device, img_np, wrist_np, state, task_desc,\n                    norm_stats, unnorm_key, preprocessor, sequence_length,\n                )\n                for i in range(min(NUM_OPEN_LOOP_STEPS, len(chunk))):\n                    action_queue.append(chunk[i])\n\n            raw_action = action_queue.popleft()\n            action = process_action(raw_action)\n False, step\n\n        if done or info.get(\"success\", False):\n            return True, step + 1\n\n    return False, max_steps\n\n\ndef evaluate(args):\n    set_seed(args.seed)\n    device = torch.device(f\"cuda:{args.gpu}\")\n\n    print(f\"[a1-eval] benchmark={args.benchmark}, n_rollouts={args.n_rollouts}, seed={args.seed}\")\n\n    model = load_a1_model(args.checkpoint, device, {unnorm_key} not in norm_stats\"\n\n    preprocessor = build_mm_preprocessor(\n        model_config=model.config,\n        shuffle_messages=True,\n        require_image_features=True,\n        is_training=False,\n    )\n\n    max_steps = SUITE_MAX_STEPS[args.benchmark]\n    libero_suite_name = SUITE_TO_LIBERO[args.benchmark]\n\n    benchmark_dict = benchmark.get_benchmark_dict()\n    task_suite = benchmark_dict[libero_suite_name]()\n    num_tasks = task_suite.n_tasks\n\n    print(f\"[a1-eval] suite={libero_suite_name}, tasks={num_tasks}, max_steps={max_steps}\")\n\n    from libero.libero.envs import OffScreenRenderEnv\n    results = {}\n    total_success = 0\n    total_episodes = 0\n\n    for task_id in range(num_tasks):\n        task = task_suite.get_task(task_id)\n        task_name = task.name\n        task_desc = task.language\n        init_states = task_suite.get_task_init_states(task_id)\n\n        env_args = {\n            \"bddl_file_name\": task.bddl_file,\n            \"camera_heights\": 256,\n            \"camera_widths\": 256,\n            \"has_renderer\": False,\n            \"has_offscreen_renderer\": True,\n            \"use_camera_obs\": True,\n            \"camera_names\": [\"agentview\", \"robot0_eye_in_hand\"],\n        }\n        env = OffScreenRenderEnv(**env_args)\n\n        task_successes = 0\n        for trial in range(args.n_rollouts):\n            set_seed(args.seed + trial)\n            init_state = init_states[trial % len(init_states)]\n\n            success, steps = run_rollout(\n                env, init_state, model, device, task_desc, max_steps,\n                norm_stats, unnorm_key, preprocessor, args.sequence_length,\n            )\n            task_successes += int(success)\n            total_success += int(success)\n            total_episodes += 1\n\n            status = \"OK\" if success else \"FAIL\"\n            print(f\"  task {task_id}/{num_tasks} [{task_name}] \"\n                  f\"trial {trial+1}/{args.n_rollouts}: {status} ({steps} steps)\")\n\n        task_sr = task_successes / args.n_rollouts\n        results[task_name] = {\"success_rate\": task_sr, \"successes\": task_successes, \"trials\": args.n_rollouts}\n        print(f\"[a1-eval] Task {task_id} [{task_name}]: SR={task_sr:.1%} ({task_successes}/{args.n_rollouts})\")\n\n        env.close()\n\n    overall_sr = total_success / total_episodes if total_episodes > 0 else 0.0\n    print(f\"\\n[a1-eval] Overall: SR={overall_sr:.1%} ({total_success}/{total_episodes})\")\n\n    summary = {\n        \"model\": \"a1-libero\",\n        \"benchmark\": args.benchmark,\n        \"mode\": \"vla_only\",\n        \"seed\": args.seed,\n        \"n_rollouts\": args.n_rollouts,\n        \"overall_success_rate\": overall_sr,\n out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    out_path.write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=2))\n    print(f\"[a1-eval] Results saved to {args.output_file}\")\n    return summary\n\n\ndef VLA baseline evaluation on LIBERO.\nStandalone script — no tensorflow dependency.\n\nUsage:\n  CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 python eval_a1_baseline.py \\\n      --benchmark object --n_rollouts 20 --seed 42 \\\n      --checkpoint /root/autodl-tmp/models/a1-libero/\n\"\"\"\nimport argparse\nimport functools\nbuiltins_print = print\nprint = functools.partial(builtins_print, flush=True)\nimport os\nos.environ.setdefault(\"DATA_DIR\", \"/tmp\")\nos.environ.setdefault(\"VLA_CONFIG_YAML\", \"libero_simulation.yaml\")\nimport sys\nimport json\nimport time\nimport traceback\nfrom collections import deque\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport torch\nimport numpy as np\nfrom PIL import Image\nfrom robosuite.utils.transform_utils import quat2axisangle\n\nfrom a1.vla.affordvla import AffordVLA\nfrom a1.config import ModelConfig\nfrom a1.util import resource_path\nfrom a1.data import build_mm_preprocessor\nfrom a1.data.collator import MMCollatorForAction\nfrom a1.vla.constants import NormalizationType\n\nfrom libero.libero import benchmark\n\nSUITE_MAX_STEPS = {\"object\": 280, \"spatial\": 280, \"goal\": 300, \"long\": 520, \"90\": 400}\nSUITE_TO_LIBERO = {\n    \"object\": \"libero_object\",\n    \"spatial\": \"libero_spatial\",\n    \"goal\": \"libero_goal\",\n    \"long\": \"libero_10\",\n    \"90\": \"libero_90\",\n}\nUNNORM_KEY_MAP = {\n    \"object\": \"libero_object_no_noops\",\n    \"spatial\": \"libero_spatial_no_noops\",\n    \"goal\": \"libero_goal_no_noops\",\n    \"long\": \"libero_10_no_noops\",\n    \"90\": \"libero_90_no_noops\",\n}\nNUM_OPEN_LOOP_STEPS = 8\nNUM_STEPS_WAIT = 10\nNORMALIZATION_TYPE = NormalizationType.BOUNDS_Q99\n\n\ndef set_seed(seed):\n    import random\n    torch.manual_seed(seed)\n    torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(seed)\n    np.random.seed(seed)\n    random.seed(seed)\n    torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True\n    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False\n    os.environ[\"PYTHONHASHSEED\"] = str(seed)\n\n\ndef load_a1_model(checkpoint_path, device, tokenizer_path=None):\n    cfg_path = resource_path(Path(checkpoint_path), \"config.yaml\")\n    model_cfg = ModelConfig.load(cfg_path, key=\"model\", validate_paths=False)\n    model_cfg.num_diffusion_inference_steps = 10\n    model_cfg.llm_causal_attention = False\n\n    if tokenizer_path:\n        model_cfg.tokenizer.identifier = tokenizer_path\n\n    model = AffordVLA(model_cfg)\n    state_dict_path = resource_path(Path(checkpoint_path), \"model.pt\")\n    state_dict = torch.load(str(state_dict_path), map_location=\"cpu\")\n    model.load_state_dict(state_dict, strict=True)\n    del state_dict\n    torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n\n    model = model.to(torch.bfloat16)\n    model.to(device)\n    model.eval()\n    print(f\"[a1] Model loaded, params={model.num_params():,d}, device={device}\")\n    return model\n\n\ndef load_norm_stats(checkpoint_path):\n    stats_path = os.path.join(checkpoint_path, \"dataset_statistics.json\")\n    with open(stats_path) as f:\n        return json.load(f)\n\n\ndef normalize_proprio_bounds_q99(proprio, stats):\n    q99 = np.array(stats[\"q99\"])\n    q01 = np.array(stats[\"q01\"])\n    mask = np.array(stats.get(\"mask\", np.ones_like(q01, dtype=bool)))\n    return np.where(mask, 2.0 * (proprio - q01) / (q99 - q01 + 1e-8) - 1.0, proprio)\n\n\ndef unnormalize_actions_bounds_q99(normalized, stats):\n    q99 = np.array(stats[\"q99\"])\n    q01 = np.array(stats[\"q01\"])\n    mask = np.array(stats[\"mask\"])\n    return np.where(mask, 0.5 * (normalized + 1) * (q99 - q01) + q01, normalized)\n\n\ndef get_libero_image(obs):\n    return obs[\"agentview_image\"][::-1]\n\n\ndef get_libero_wrist_image(obs):\n    return obs[\"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\"][::-1]\n\n\ndef prepare_obs(obs, resize_size):\n    img = get_libero_image(obs)\n    wrist = get_libero_wrist_image(obs)\n    img_pil = Image.fromarray(img).resize(resize_size, Image.BILINEAR)\n    wrist_pil = Image.fromarray(wrist).resize(resize_size, Image.BILINEAR)\n    state = np.concatenate([\n        obs[\"robot0_eef_pos\"],\n        quat2axisangle(obs[\"robot0_eef_quat\"]),\n        obs[\"robot0_gripper_qpos\"],\n    ])\n    return np.array(img_pil), np.array(wrist_pil), state\n\n\n@torch.no_grad()\ndef predict_actions(model, device, img_np, wrist_np, state, task_desc,\n                    norm_stats, unnorm_key, preprocessor, sequence_length):\n    proprio = torch.tensor(state, dtype=torch.float32).unsqueeze(0)\n    gripper_raw = proprio[..., -1]\n    gripper_norm = torch.clamp(2.0 * (gripper_raw - 0.02) / (0.04 + 1e-8) - 1.0, -1.0, 1.0)\n\n    proprio_np = proprio[..., :-1].numpy()\n    proprio_stats = norm_stats[unnorm_key][\"proprio\"]\n    proprio_np = normalize_proprio_bounds_q99(proprio_np, proprio_stats)\n    proprio[..., :-1] = torch.from_numpy(proprio_np).float()\n    proprio[..., -1] = gripper_norm\n\n    proprio = proprio.unsqueeze(1).numpy()\n    pad_width = [(0, 0)] * (proprio.ndim - 1) + [(0, model.config.proprio_dim - proprio.shape[-1])]\n    proprio = np.pad(proprio, pad_width, constant_values=0)\n\n    dummy_action = np.zeros((model.config.num_actions_chunk, model.config.action_dim), dtype=np.float32)\n    input_data = {\n        \"question\": task_desc,\n        \"proprio\": proprio,\n        \"action\": dummy_action,\n        \"action_pad_mask\": np.zeros_like(dummy_action, dtype=bool),\n        \"answer\": \"Action\",\n        \"style\": \"action\",\n        \"metadata\": {},\n        \"images\": [Image.fromarray(img_np), Image.fromarray(wrist_np)],\n    }\n\n    processed = preprocessor(input_data)\n\n    collator = MMCollatorForAction(\n        model_config=model.config,\n        use_proprio=True,\n        max_sequence_length=sequence_length,\n        include_metadata=False,\n        pad=\"to_max\",\n        max_crops=model.config.get_max_crops(),\n    )\n    batch = collator([processed])\n    for k in batch:\n        if isinstance(batch[k], torch.Tensor):\n            batch[k] = batch[k].to(device)\n\n    model_inputs = {\n        \"input_ids\": batch[\"input_ids\"],\n        \"images\": batch.get(\"images\"),\n        \"image_masks\": batch.get(\"image_masks\"),\n        \"attention_mask\": batch.get(\"attention_mask\"),\n        \"attention_bias\": batch.get(\"attention_bias\"),\n        \"response_mask\": (batch[\"loss_masks\"] > 0) if \"loss_masks\" in batch else None,\n        \"image_input_idx\": batch.get(\"image_input_idx\"),\n        \"subsegment_ids\": batch.get(\"subsegment_ids\"),\n        \"position_ids\": batch.get(\"position_ids\"),\n        \"action_proprio\": batch.get(\"proprio\"),\n        \"proprio_token_idx\": batch.get(\"proprio_token_idx\"),\n        \"output_hidden_states\": False,\n        \"use_cache\": True,\n    }\n\n    norm_actions = model.predict_actions(**model_inputs)\n    norm_actions = norm_actions.to(torch.float32).cpu().numpy()\n    norm_actions = norm_actions[..., :7]\n\n    action_stats = norm_stats[unnorm_key][\"action\"]\n    actions = unnormalize_actions_bounds_q99(norm_actions, action_stats)\n\n    if actions.ndim == 3:\n        actions = actions[0]\n    return actions\n\n\ndef process_action(action):\n    a = action.copy()\n    a[-1] = 1.0 if a[-1] > 0.5 else -1.0\n    a[-1] = -a[-1]\n    return a\n\n\ndef run_rollout(env, init_state, model, device, task_desc, max_steps,\n                norm_stats, unnorm_key, preprocessor, sequence_length):\n    env.reset()\n    obs = env.set_init_state(init_state)\n\n    for _ in range(NUM_STEPS_WAIT):\n        obs, _, _, _ = env.step(np.array([0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, -1], dtype=np.float64))\n\n    resize_size = tuple(model.config.vision_backbone.image_default_input_size)\n    action_queue = deque()\n\n    for step in range(max_steps):\n        try:\n            if len(action_queue) == 0:\n                img_np, wrist_np, state = prepare_obs(obs, resize_size)\n                chunk = predict_actions(\n                    model, device, img_np, wrist_np, state, task_desc,\n                    norm_stats, unnorm_key, preprocessor, sequence_length,\n                )\n                for i in range(min(NUM_OPEN_LOOP_STEPS, len(chunk))):\n                    action_queue.append(chunk[i])\n\n            raw_action = action_queue.popleft()\n            action = process_action(raw_action)\n            obs, reward, done, info = env.step(action.tolist())\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"[rollout] step {step}: {e}\")\n            traceback.print_exc()\n            return False, step\n\n        if done or info.get(\"success\", False):\n            return True, step + 1\n\n    return False, max_steps\n\n\ndef evaluate(args):\n    set_seed(args.seed)\n    device = torch.device(f\"cuda:{args.gpu}\")\n\n    print(f\"[a1-eval] benchmark={args.benchmark}, n_rollouts={args.n_rollouts}, seed={args.seed}\")\n\n    model = load_a1_model(args.checkpoint, device, tokenizer_path=getattr(args, 'tokenizer_path', None))\n    norm_stats = load_norm_stats(args.checkpoint)\n    unnorm_key = UNNORM_KEY_MAP[args.benchmark]\n    assert unnorm_key in norm_stats, f\"Key {unnorm_key} not in norm_stats\"\n\n    preprocessor = build_mm_preprocessor(\n        model_config=model.config,\n        shuffle_messages=True,\n        require_image_features=True,\n        is_training=False,\n    )\n\n    max_steps = SUITE_MAX_STEPS[args.benchmark]\n    libero_suite_name = SUITE_TO_LIBERO[args.benchmark]\n\n    benchmark_dict = benchmark.get_benchmark_dict()\n    task_suite = benchmark_dict[libero_suite_name]()\n    num_tasks = task_suite.n_tasks\n\n    print(f\"[a1-eval] suite={libero_suite_name}, tasks={num_tasks}, max_steps={max_steps}\")\n\n    from libero.libero.envs import OffScreenRenderEnv\n    results = {}\n    total_success = 0\n    total_episodes = 0\n\n    for task_id in range(num_tasks):\n        task = task_suite.get_task(task_id)\n        task_name = task.name\n        task_desc = task.language\n        init_states = task_suite.get_task_init_states(task_id)\n\n        env_args = {\n            \"bddl_file_name\": task.bddl_file,\n            \"camera_heights\": 256,\n            \"camera_widths\": 256,\n            \"has_renderer\": False,\n            \"has_offscreen_renderer\": True,\n            \"use_camera_obs\": True,\n            \"camera_names\": [\"agentview\", \"robot0_eye_in_hand\"],\n        }\n        env = OffScreenRenderEnv(**env_args)\n\n        task_successes = 0\n        for trial in range(args.n_rollouts):\n            set_seed(args.seed + trial)\n            init_state = init_states[trial % len(init_states)]\n\n            success, steps = run_rollout(\n                env, init_state, model, device, task_desc, max_steps,\n                norm_stats, unnorm_key, preprocessor, args.sequence_length,\n            )\n            task_successes += int(success)\n            total_success += int(success)\n            total_episodes += 1\n\n            status = \"OK\" if success else \"FAIL\"\n            print(f\"  task {task_id}/{num_tasks} [{task_name}] \"\n                  f\"trial {trial+1}/{args.n_rollouts}: {status} ({steps} steps)\")\n\n        task_sr = task_successes / args.n_rollouts\n        results[task_name] = {\"success_rate\": task_sr, \"successes\": task_successes, \"trials\": args.n_rollouts}\n        print(f\"[a1-eval] Task {task_id} [{task_name}]: SR={task_sr:.1%} ({task_successes}/{args.n_rollouts})\")\n\n        env.close()\n\n    overall_sr = total_success / total_episodes if total_episodes > 0 else 0.0\n    print(f\"\\n[a1-eval] Overall: SR={overall_sr:.1%} ({total_success}/{total_episodes})\")\n\n    summary = {\n        \"model\": \"a1-libero\",\n        \"benchmark\": args.benchmark,\n        \"mode\": \"vla_only\",\n        \"seed\": args.seed,\n        \"n_rollouts\": args.n_rollouts,\n        \"overall_success_rate\": overall_sr,\n        \"total_successes\": total_success,\n        \"total_episodes\": total_episodes,\n        \"per_task\": results,\n    }\n\n    out_path = Path(args.output_file)\n    out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    out_path.write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=2))\n    print(f\"[a1-eval] Results saved to {args.output_file}\")\n    return summary\n\n\ndef main():\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    p.add_argument(\"--benchmark\", default=\"object\", choices=[\"object\", \"spatial\", \"goal\", \"long\", \"90\"])\n    p.add_argument(\"--checkpoint\", default=\"/root/autodl-tmp/models/a1-libero/\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--n_rollouts\", type=int, default=20)\n    p.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    p.add_argument(\"--gpu\", type=int, default=0)\n    p.add_argument(\"--sequence_length\", type=int, default=1024)\n    p.add_argument(\"--output_file\", default=None)\n    p.add_argument(\"--tokenizer_path\", default=None)\n    args = p.parse_args()\n\n    if args.output_file is None:\n        args.output_file = f\"logs/eval_{args.benchmark}_a1_vla_only_seed{args.seed}.json\"\n\n    evaluate(args)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","numLines":362,"startLine":1,"totalLines":362}}}

proprio.shape[-1])]\n    proprio = np.pad(proprio, pad_width, constant_values=0)\n\n    dummy_action = np.zeros((model.config.num_actions_chunk, model.config.action_dim), dtype=np.float32)\n    input_data = {\n        \"question\": task_desc,\n        \"proprio\": proprio,\n        \"action\": dummy_action,\n        \"action_pad_mask\": np.zeros_like(dummy_action, dtype=bool),\n        \"answer\": \"Action\",\n        \"style\": \"action\",\n        \"metadata\": {},\n        \"images\": [Image.fromarray(img_np), Image.fromarray(wrist_np)],\n    }\n\n    processed = preprocessor(input_data)\n\n    collator = MMCollatorForAction(\n        model_config=model.config,\n        use_proprio=True,\n        max_sequence_length=sequence_length,\n        include_metadata=False,\n        pad=\"to_max\",\n        max_crops=model.config.get_max_crops(),\n    )\n    batch = collator([processed])\n    for k in batch:\n        if isinstance(batch[k], torch.Tensor):\n            batch[k] = batch[k].to(device)\n\n    model_inputs = {\n        \"input_ids\": batch[\"input_ids\"],\n        \"images\": batch.get(\"images\"),\n        \"image_masks\": batch.get(\"image_masks\"),\n        \"attention_mask\": batch.get(\"attention_mask\"),\n        \"attention_bias\": batch.get(\"attention_bias\"),\n        \"response_mask\": (batch[\"loss_masks\"] > 0) if \"loss_masks\" in batch else None,\n        \"image_input_idx\": batch.get(\"image_input_idx\"),\n        \"subsegment_ids\": batch.get(\"subsegment_ids\"),\n        \"position_ids\": batch.get(\"position_ids\"),\n        \"action_proprio\": batch.get(\"proprio\"),\n        \"proprio_token_idx\": batch.get(\"proprio_token_idx\"),\n        \"output_hidden_states\": False,\n        \"use_cache\": True,\n    }\n\n    norm_actions = model.predict_actions(**model_inputs)\n    norm_actions = norm_actions.to(torch.float32).cpu().numpy()\n    norm_actions = norm_actions[..., :7]\n\n    action_stats = norm_stats[unnorm_key][\"action\"]\n    actions = unnormalize_actions_bounds_q99(norm_actions, action_stats)\n\n    if actions.ndim == 3:\n        actions = actions[0]\n    return actions\n\n\ndef process_action(action):\n    a = action.copy()\n    a[-1] = 1.0 if a[-1] > 0.5 else -1.0\n    a[-1] = -a[-1]\n    return a\n\n\ndef run_rollout(env, init_state, model, device, task_desc, max_steps,\n                norm_stats, unnorm_key, preprocessor, sequence_length):\n    env.reset()\n    obs = env.set_init_state(init_state)\n\n    for _ in range(NUM_STEPS_WAIT):\n        obs, _, _, _ = env.step(np.array([0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, -1], dtype=np.float64))\n\n    resize_size = tuple(model.config.vision_backbone.image_default_input_size)\n    action_queue = deque()\n\n    for step in range(max_steps):\n        try:\n            if len(action_queue) == 0:\n                img_np, wrist_np, state = prepare_obs(obs, resize_size)\n                chunk = predict_actions(\n                    model, device, img_np, wrist_np, state, task_desc,\n                    norm_stats, unnorm_key, preprocessor, sequence_length,\n                )\n                for i in range(min(NUM_OPEN_LOOP_STEPS, len(chunk))):\n                    action_queue.append(chunk[i])\n\n            raw_action = action_queue.popleft()\n            action = process_action(raw_action)\n            obs, reward, done, info = env.step(action.tolist())\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"[rollout] step {step}: {e}\")\n            traceback.print_exc()\n            return False, step\n\n        if done or info.get(\"success\", False):\n            return True, step + 1\n\n    return False, max_steps\n\n\ndef evaluate(args):\n    set_seed(args.seed)\n    device = torch.device(f\"cuda:{args.gpu}\")\n\n    print(f\"[a1-eval] benchmark={args.benchmark}, n_rollouts={args.n_rollouts}, seed={args.seed}\")\n\n    model = load_a1_model(args.checkpoint, device, tokenizer_path=getattr(args, 'tokenizer_path', None))\n    norm_stats = load_norm_stats(args.checkpoint)\n    unnorm_key = UNNORM_KEY_MAP[args.benchmark]\n    assert unnorm_key in norm_stats, f\"Key {unnorm_key} not in norm_stats\"\n\n    preprocessor = build_mm_preprocessor(\n        model_config=model.config,\n        shuffle_messages=True,\n        require_image_features=True,\n        is_training=False,\n    )\n\n    max_steps = SUITE_MAX_STEPS[args.benchmark]\n    libero_suite_name = SUITE_TO_LIBERO[args.benchmark]\n\n    benchmark_dict = benchmark.get_benchmark_dict()\n    task_suite = benchmark_dict[libero_suite_name]()\n    num_tasks = task_suite.n_tasks\n\n    print(f\"[a1-eval] suite={libero_suite_name}, tasks={num_tasks}, max_steps={max_steps}\")\n\n    from libero.libero.envs import OffScreenRenderEnv\n    results = {}\n    total_success = 0\n    total_episodes = 0\n\n    for task_id in range(num_tasks):\n        task = task_suite.get_task(task_id)\n        task_name = task.name\n        task_desc = task.language\n        init_states = task_suite.get_task_init_states(task_id)\n\n        env_args = {\n            \"bddl_file_name\": task.bddl_file,\n            \"camera_heights\": 256,\n            \"camera_widths\": 256,\n            \"has_renderer\": False,\n            \"has_offscreen_renderer\": True,\n            \"use_camera_obs\": True,\n            \"camera_names\": [\"agentview\", \"robot0_eye_in_hand\"],\n        }\n        env = OffScreenRenderEnv(**env_args)\n\n        task_successes = 0\n        for trial in range(args.n_rollouts):\n            set_seed(args.seed + trial)\n            init_state = init_states[trial % len(init_states)]\n\n            success, steps = run_rollout(\n                env, init_state, model, device, task_desc, max_steps,\n                norm_stats, unnorm_key, preprocessor, args.sequence_length,\n            )\n            task_successes += int(success)\n            total_success += int(success)\n            total_episodes += 1\n\n            status = \"OK\" if success else \"FAIL\"\n            print(f\"  task {task_id}/{num_tasks} [{task_name}] \"\n                  f\"trial {trial+1}/{args.n_rollouts}: {status} ({steps} steps)\")\n\n        task_sr = task_successes / args.n_rollouts\n        results[task_name] = {\"success_rate\": task_sr, \"successes\": task_successes, \"trials\": args.n_rollouts}\n        print(f\"[a1-eval] Task {task_id} [{task_name}]: SR={task_sr:.1%} ({task_successes}/{args.n_rollouts})\")\n\n        env.close()\n\n    overall_sr = total_success / total_episodes if total_episodes > 0 else 0.0\n    print(f\"\\n[a1-eval] Overall: SR={overall_sr:.1%} ({total_success}/{total_episodes})\")\n\n    summary = {\n        \"model\": \"a1-libero\",\n        \"benchmark\": args.benchmark,\n        \"mode\": \"vla_only\",\n        \"seed\": args.seed,\n        \"n_rollouts\": args.n_rollouts,\n        \"overall_success_rate\": overall_sr,\n        \"total_successes\": total_success,\n        \"total_episodes\": total_episodes,\n        \"per_task\": results,\n    }\n\n    out_path = Path(args.output_file)\n    out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    out_path.write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=2))\n    print(f\"[a1-eval] Results saved to {args.output_file}\")\n    return summary\n\n\ndef main():\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    p.add_argument(\"--benchmark\", default=\"object\", choices=[\"object\", \"spatial\", \"goal\", \"long\", \"90\"])\n    p.add_argument(\"--checkpoint\", default=\"/root/autodl-tmp/models/a1-libero/\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--n_rollouts\", type=int, default=20)\n    p.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    p.add_argument(\"--gpu\", type=int, default=0)\n    p.add_argument(\"--sequence_length\", type=int, default=1024)\n    p.add_argument(\"--output_file\", default=None)\n    p.add_argument(\"--tokenizer_path\", default=None)\n    args = p.parse_args()\n\n    if args.output_file is None:\n        args.output_file = f\"logs/eval_{args.benchmark}_a1_vla_only_seed{args.seed}.json\"\n\n    evaluate(args)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\nPYEOF\necho \"DONE: quat2axisangle\n\nfrom a1.vla.affordvla import AffordVLA\nfrom a1.config import ModelConfig\nfrom a1.util import resource_path\nfrom a1.data import build_mm_preprocessor\nfrom a1.data.collator import \"libero_90\",\n}\nUNNORM_KEY_MAP = {\n    \"object\": \"libero_object_no_noops\",\n    \"spatial\": \"libero_spatial_no_noops\",\n    \"goal\": \"libero_goal_no_noops\",\n    \"long\": \"libero_10_no_noops\",\n    \"90\": \"libero_90_no_noops\",\n}\nNUM_OPEN_LOOP_STEPS = 8\nNUM_STEPS_WAIT = 10\nNORMALIZATION_TYPE = NormalizationType.BOUNDS_Q99\n\n\ndef set_seed(seed):\n    import random\n    torch.manual_seed(seed)\n    torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(seed)\n    np.random.seed(seed)\n    random.seed(seed)\n    torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True\n    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False\n    os.environ[\"PYTHONHASHSEED\"] = str(seed)\n\n\ndef load_a1_model(checkpoint_path, device, tokenizer_path=None):\n    cfg_path = resource_path(Path(checkpoint_path), \"config.yaml\")\n    model_cfg = ModelConfig.load(cfg_path, key=\"model\", validate_paths=False)\n    model_cfg.num_diffusion_inference_steps = 10\n    model_cfg.llm_causal_attention = False\n\n    if tokenizer_path:\n        model_cfg.tokenizer.identifier = tokenizer_path\n\n    model = AffordVLA(model_cfg)\n    state_dict_path = resource_path(Path(checkpoint_path), \"model.pt\")\n    state_dict = torch.load(str(state_dict_path), map_location=\"cpu\")\n    model.load_state_dict(state_dict, strict=True)\n    del state_dict\n    torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n\n    model = model.to(torch.bfloat16)\n    model.to(device)\n    model.eval()\n    print(f\"[a1] Model loaded, params={model.num_params():,d}, device={device}\")\n    return model\n\n\ndef load_norm_stats(checkpoint_path):\n    stats_path = os.path.join(checkpoint_path, \"dataset_statistics.json\")\n    with open(stats_path) as f:\n        return json.load(f)\n\n\ndef normalize_proprio_bounds_q99(proprio, stats):\n    q99 = np.array(stats[\"q99\"])\n    q01 = np.array(stats[\"q01\"])\n    mask = np.array(stats.get(\"mask\", np.ones_like(q01, dtype=bool)))\n    return np.where(mask, 2.0 * (proprio - q01) / (q99 - q01 + 1e-8) - 1.0, proprio)\n\n\ndef unnormalize_actions_bounds_q99(normalized, stats):\n    q99 = np.array(stats[\"q99\"])\n    q01 = np.array(stats[\"q01\"])\n    mask = np.array(stats[\"mask\"])\n    return np.where(mask, 0.5 * (normalized + 1) * (q99 - q01) + q01, normalized)\n\n\ndef get_libero_image(obs):\n    return obs[\"agentview_image\"][::-1]\n\n\ndef get_libero_wrist_image(obs):\n    return obs[\"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\"][::-1]\n\n\ndef prepare_obs(obs, resize_size):\n    img = get_libero_image(obs)\n    wrist = get_libero_wrist_image(obs)\n    img_pil = Image.fromarray(img).resize(resize_size, Image.BILINEAR)\n    wrist_pil = Image.fromarray(wrist).resize(resize_size, Image.BILINEAR)\n    state = np.concatenate([\n        obs[\"robot0_eef_pos\"],\n        quat2axisangle(obs[\"robot0_eef_quat\"]),\n        obs[\"robot0_gripper_qpos\"],\n    ])\n    return np.array(img_pil), np.array(wrist_pil), state\n\n\n@torch.no_grad()\ndef predict_actions(model, device, img_np, wrist_np, state, task_desc,\n                    norm_stats, unnorm_key, preprocessor, sequence_length):\n    proprio = torch.tensor(state, dtype=torch.float32).unsqueeze(0)\n    gripper_raw = proprio[..., -1]\n    gripper_norm = torch.clamp(2.0 * (gripper_raw - 0.02) / (0.04 + 1e-8) - 1.0, -1.0, 1.0)\n\n    proprio_np = proprio[..., :-1].numpy()\n    proprio_stats = norm_stats[unnorm_key][\"proprio\"]\n    proprio_np = normalize_proprio_bounds_q99(proprio_np, proprio_stats)\n    proprio[..., :-1] = torch.from_numpy(proprio_np).float()\n    proprio[..., -1] = gripper_norm\n\n    proprio = proprio.unsqueeze(1).numpy()\n    pad_width = [(0, 0)] * (proprio.ndim - 1) + [(0, model.config.proprio_dim - proprio.shape[-1])]\n    proprio = np.pad(proprio, pad_width, constant_values=0)\n\n    dummy_action = np.zeros((model.config.num_actions_chunk, model.config.action_dim), dtype=np.float32)\n    input_data = {\n        \"question\": task_desc,\n        \"proprio\": proprio,\n        \"action\": dummy_action,\n        \"action_pad_mask\": np.zeros_like(dummy_action, dtype=bool),\n        \"answer\": \"Action\",\n        \"style\": \"action\",\n        \"metadata\": {},\n        \"images\": [Image.fromarray(img_np), Image.fromarray(wrist_np)],\n    }\n\n    processed = preprocessor(input_data)\n\n    collator = MMCollatorForAction(\n        model_config=model.config,\n        use_proprio=True,\n        max_sequence_length=sequence_length,\n        include_metadata=False,\n        pad=\"to_max\",\n        max_crops=model.config.get_max_crops(),\n    )\n    batch = collator([processed])\n    for k in batch:\n        if isinstance(batch[k], torch.Tensor):\n            batch[k] = batch[k].to(device)\n\n    model_inputs = {\n        \"input_ids\": batch[\"input_ids\"],\n        \"images\": batch.get(\"images\"),\n        \"image_masks\": batch.get(\"image_masks\"),\n        \"attention_mask\": batch.get(\"attention_mask\"),\n        \"attention_bias\": batch.get(\"attention_bias\"),\n        \"response_mask\": (batch[\"loss_masks\"] > 0) if \"loss_masks\" in batch else None,\n        \"image_input_idx\": batch.get(\"image_input_idx\"),\n        \"subsegment_ids\": batch.get(\"subsegment_ids\"),\n        \"position_ids\": batch.get(\"position_ids\"),\n        \"action_proprio\": batch.get(\"proprio\"),\n        \"proprio_token_idx\": batch.get(\"proprio_token_idx\"),\n        \"output_hidden_states\": False,\n        \"use_cache\": True,\n    }\n\n    norm_actions = model.predict_actions(**model_inputs)\n    norm_actions = norm_actions.to(torch.float32).cpu().numpy()\n    norm_actions = norm_actions[..., :7]\n\n    action_stats = norm_stats[unnorm_key][\"action\"]\n    actions = unnormalize_actions_bounds_q99(norm_actions, action_stats)\n\n    if actions.ndim == 3:\n        actions = actions[0]\n    return actions\n\n\ndef process_action(action):\n    a = action.copy()\n    a[-1] = 1.0 if a[-1] > 0.5 else -1.0\n    a[-1] = -a[-1]\n    return a\n\n\ndef run_rollout(env, init_state, model, device, task_desc, max_steps,\n                norm_stats, unnorm_key, preprocessor, sequence_length):\n    env.reset()\n    obs = env.set_init_state(init_state)\n\n    for _ in range(NUM_STEPS_WAIT):\n        obs, _, _, _ = env.step(np.array([0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, -1], dtype=np.float64))\n\n    resize_size = tuple(model.config.vision_backbone.image_default_input_size)\n    action_queue = deque()\n\n    for step in range(max_steps):\n        try:\n            if len(action_queue) == 0:\n                img_np, wrist_np, state = prepare_obs(obs, resize_size)\n                chunk = predict_actions(\n                    model, device, img_np, wrist_np, state, task_desc,\n                    norm_stats, unnorm_key, preprocessor, sequence_length,\n                )\n                for i in range(min(NUM_OPEN_LOOP_STEPS, len(chunk))):\n                    action_queue.append(chunk[i])\n\n            raw_action = action_queue.popleft()\n            action = process_action(raw_action)\n            obs, reward, done, info = env.step(action.tolist())\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"[rollout] step {step}: {e}\")\n            traceback.print_exc()\n            return False, step\n\n        if done or info.get(\"success\", False):\n            return True, step + 1\n\n    return False, max_steps\n\n\ndef evaluate(args):\n    set_seed(args.seed)\n    device = torch.device(f\"cuda:{args.gpu}\")\n\n    print(f\"[a1-eval] benchmark={args.benchmark}, n_rollouts={args.n_rollouts}, seed={args.seed}\")\n\n    model = load_a1_model(args.checkpoint, device, tokenizer_path=getattr(args, 'tokenizer_path', None))\n    norm_stats = load_norm_stats(args.checkpoint)\n    unnorm_key = UNNORM_KEY_MAP[args.benchmark]\n    assert unnorm_key in norm_stats, f\"Key {unnorm_key} not in norm_stats\"\n\n    preprocessor = build_mm_preprocessor(\n        model_config=model.config,\n        shuffle_messages=True,\n        require_image_features=True,\n object --n_rollouts 20 --seed 42 \\\n      --checkpoint /root/autodl-tmp/models/a1-libero/\n\"\"\"\nimport argparse\nimport functools\nbuiltins_print = print\nprint = functools.partial(builtins_print, flush=True)\nimport os\nos.environ.setdefault(\"DATA_DIR\", \"/tmp\")\nos.environ.setdefault(\"VLA_CONFIG_YAML\", \"libero_simulation.yaml\")\nimport sys\nimport json\nimport time\nimport traceback\nfrom collections import deque\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport torch\nimport numpy as np\nfrom PIL import Image\nfrom robosuite.utils.transform_utils import quat2axisangle\n\nfrom a1.vla.affordvla import AffordVLA\nfrom a1.config import ModelConfig\nfrom a1.util import resource_path\nfrom a1.data import build_mm_preprocessor\nfrom a1.data.collator import MMCollatorForAction\nfrom a1.vla.constants import NormalizationType\n\nfrom libero.libero import benchmark\n\nSUITE_MAX_STEPS = {\"object\": 280, \"spatial\": 280, \"goal\": 300, \"long\": 520, \"90\": 400}\nSUITE_TO_LIBERO = {\n    \"object\": \"libero_object\",\n    \"spatial\": \"libero_spatial\",\n    \"goal\": \"libero_goal\",\n    \"long\": \"libero_10\",\n    \"90\": \"libero_90\",\n}\nUNNORM_KEY_MAP = {\n    \"object\": \"libero_object_no_noops\",\n    \"spatial\": \"libero_spatial_no_noops\",\n    \"goal\": \"libero_goal_no_noops\",\n    \"long\": \"libero_10_no_noops\",\n    \"90\": \"libero_90_no_noops\",\n}\nNUM_OPEN_LOOP_STEPS = 8\nNUM_STEPS_WAIT = 10\nNORMALIZATION_TYPE = NormalizationType.BOUNDS_Q99\n\n\ndef set_seed(seed):\n    import random\n    torch.manual_seed(seed)\n    torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(seed)\n    np.random.seed(seed)\n    random.seed(seed)\n    torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True\n    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False\n    os.environ[\"PYTHONHASHSEED\"] = str(seed)\n\n\ndef load_a1_model(checkpoint_path, device, tokenizer_path=None):\n    cfg_path = resource_path(Path(checkpoint_path), \"config.yaml\")\n    model_cfg = ModelConfig.load(cfg_path, key=\"model\", validate_paths=False)\n    model_cfg.num_diffusion_inference_steps = 10\n    model_cfg.llm_causal_attention = False\n\n    if tokenizer_path:\n        model_cfg.tokenizer.identifier = tokenizer_path\n\n    model = AffordVLA(model_cfg)\n    state_dict_path = resource_path(Path(checkpoint_path), \"model.pt\")\n    state_dict = torch.load(str(state_dict_path), map_location=\"cpu\")\n    model.load_state_dict(state_dict, strict=True)\n    del state_dict\n    torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n\n    model = model.to(torch.bfloat16)\n    model.to(device)\n    model.eval()\n    print(f\"[a1] Model loaded, params={model.num_params():,d}, device={device}\")\n    return model\n\n\ndef load_norm_stats(checkpoint_path):\n    stats_path = os.path.join(checkpoint_path, \"dataset_statistics.json\")\n    with open(stats_path) as f:\n        return json.load(f)\n\n\ndef normalize_proprio_bounds_q99(proprio, stats):\n    q99 = np.array(stats[\"q99\"])\n    q01 = np.array(stats[\"q01\"])\n    mask = np.array(stats.get(\"mask\", np.ones_like(q01, dtype=bool)))\n    return np.where(mask, 2.0 * (proprio - q01) / (q99 - q01 + 1e-8) - 1.0, proprio)\n\n\ndef unnormalize_actions_bounds_q99(normalized, stats):\n    q99 = np.array(stats[\"q99\"])\n    q01 = np.array(stats[\"q01\"])\n    mask = np.array(stats[\"mask\"])\n    return np.where(mask, 0.5 * (normalized + 1) * (q99 - q01) + q01, normalized)\n\n\ndef get_libero_image(obs):\n    return obs[\"agentview_image\"][::-1]\n\n\ndef get_libero_wrist_image(obs):\n    return obs[\"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\"][::-1]\n\n\ndef prepare_obs(obs, resize_size):\n    img = get_libero_image(obs)\n    wrist = get_libero_wrist_image(obs)\n    img_pil = Image.fromarray(img).resize(resize_size, Image.BILINEAR)\n    wrist_pil = Image.fromarray(wrist).resize(resize_size, Image.BILINEAR)\n    state = np.concatenate([\n        obs[\"robot0_eef_pos\"],\n        quat2axisangle(obs[\"robot0_eef_quat\"]),\n        obs[\"robot0_gripper_qpos\"],\n    ])\n    return np.array(img_pil), np.array(wrist_pil), state\n\n\n@torch.no_grad()\ndef predict_actions(model, device, img_np, wrist_np, state, task_desc,\n                    norm_stats, unnorm_key, preprocessor, sequence_length):\n    proprio = torch.tensor(state, dtype=torch.float32).unsqueeze(0)\n    gripper_raw = proprio[..., -1]\n    gripper_norm = torch.clamp(2.0 * (gripper_raw - 0.02) / (0.04 + 1e-8) - 1.0, -1.0, 1.0)\n\n    proprio_np = proprio[..., :-1].numpy()\n    proprio_stats = norm_stats[unnorm_key][\"proprio\"]\n    proprio_np = normalize_proprio_bounds_q99(proprio_np, proprio_stats)\n    proprio[..., :-1] = torch.from_numpy(proprio_np).float()\n    proprio[..., -1] = gripper_norm\n\n    proprio = proprio.unsqueeze(1).numpy()\n    pad_width = [(0, 0)] * (proprio.ndim - 1) + [(0, model.config.proprio_dim - proprio.shape[-1])]\n    proprio = np.pad(proprio, pad_width, constant_values=0)\n\n    dummy_action = np.zeros((model.config.num_actions_chunk, model.config.action_dim), dtype=np.float32)\n    input_data = {\n        \"question\": task_desc,\n        \"proprio\": proprio,\n        \"action\": dummy_action,\n        \"action_pad_mask\": np.zeros_like(dummy_action, dtype=bool),\n        \"answer\": \"Action\",\n        \"style\": \"action\",\n        \"metadata\": {},\n        \"images\": [Image.fromarray(img_np), Image.fromarray(wrist_np)],\n    }\n\n    processed = preprocessor(input_data)\n\n    collator = MMCollatorForAction(\n        model_config=model.config,\n        use_proprio=True,\n        max_sequence_length=sequence_length,\n        include_metadata=False,\n        pad=\"to_max\",\n        max_crops=model.config.get_max_crops(),\n    )\n    batch = collator([processed])\n    for k in batch:\n        if isinstance(batch[k], torch.Tensor):\n            batch[k] = batch[k].to(device)\n\n    model_inputs = {\n        \"input_ids\": batch[\"input_ids\"],\n        \"images\": batch.get(\"images\"),\n        \"image_masks\": batch.get(\"image_masks\"),\n        \"attention_mask\": batch.get(\"attention_mask\"),\n        \"attention_bias\": batch.get(\"attention_bias\"),\n        \"response_mask\": (batch[\"loss_masks\"] > 0) if \"loss_masks\" in batch else None,\n        \"image_input_idx\": batch.get(\"image_input_idx\"),\n        \"subsegment_ids\": batch.get(\"subsegment_ids\"),\n        \"position_ids\": batch.get(\"position_ids\"),\n        \"action_proprio\": batch.get(\"proprio\"),\n        \"proprio_token_idx\": batch.get(\"proprio_token_idx\"),\n        \"output_hidden_states\": False,\n        \"use_cache\": True,\n    }\n\n    norm_actions = model.predict_actions(**model_inputs)\n    norm_actions = norm_actions.to(torch.float32).cpu().numpy()\n    norm_actions = norm_actions[..., :7]\n\n    action_stats = norm_stats[unnorm_key][\"action\"]\n    actions = unnormalize_actions_bounds_q99(norm_actions, action_stats)\n\n    if actions.ndim == 3:\n        actions = actions[0]\n    return actions\n\n\ndef process_action(action):\n    a = action.copy()\n    a[-1] = 1.0 if a[-1] > 0.5 else -1.0\n    a[-1] = -a[-1]\n    return a\n\n\ndef run_rollout(env, init_state, model, device, task_desc, max_steps,\n                norm_stats, unnorm_key, preprocessor, sequence_length):\n    env.reset()\n    obs = env.set_init_state(init_state)\n\n    for _ in range(NUM_STEPS_WAIT):\n        obs, _, _, _ = env.step(np.array([0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, -1], dtype=np.float64))\n\n    resize_size = tuple(model.config.vision_backbone.image_default_input_size)\n    action_queue = deque()\n\n    for step in range(max_steps):\n        try:\n            if len(action_queue) == 0:\n                img_np, wrist_np, state = prepare_obs(obs, resize_size)\n                chunk = predict_actions(\n                    model, device, img_np, wrist_np, state, task_desc,\n                    norm_stats, unnorm_key, preprocessor, sequence_length,\n                )\n                for i in range(min(NUM_OPEN_LOOP_STEPS, len(chunk))):\n                    action_queue.append(chunk[i])\n\n            raw_action = action_queue.popleft()\n            action = process_action(raw_action)\n            obs, reward, done, info = env.step(action.tolist())\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"[rollout] step {step}: {e}\")\n            traceback.print_exc()\n            return False, step\n\n        if done or info.get(\"success\", False):\n            return True, step + 1\n\n    return False, max_steps\n\n\ndef evaluate(args):\n    set_seed(args.seed)\n    device = torch.device(f\"cuda:{args.gpu}\")\n\n    print(f\"[a1-eval] benchmark={args.benchmark}, n_rollouts={args.n_rollouts}, seed={args.seed}\")\n\n    model = load_a1_model(args.checkpoint, device, tokenizer_path=getattr(args, 'tokenizer_path', None))\n    norm_stats = load_norm_stats(args.checkpoint)\n    unnorm_key = UNNORM_KEY_MAP[args.benchmark]\n    assert unnorm_key in norm_stats, f\"Key {unnorm_key} not in norm_stats\"\n\n    preprocessor = build_mm_preprocessor(\n        model_config=model.config,\n        shuffle_messages=True,\n        require_image_features=True,\n        is_training=False,\n    )\n\n    max_steps = SUITE_MAX_STEPS[args.benchmark]\n    libero_suite_name = SUITE_TO_LIBERO[args.benchmark]\n\n    task_suite = benchmark.get_benchmark(libero_suite_name)()\n    num_tasks = task_suite.n_tasks\n\n    print(f\"[a1-eval] suite={libero_suite_name}, tasks={num_tasks}, max_steps={max_steps}\")\n\n    from libero.libero.envs import OffScreenRenderEnv\n    results = {}\n    total_success = 0\n    total_episodes = 0\n\n    for task_id in range(num_tasks):\n        task = task_suite.get_task(task_id)\n        task_name = task.name\n        task_desc = task.language\n        task_bddl = task_suite.get_task_bddl_file_path(task_id)\n        init_states = task_suite.get_task_init_states(task_id)\n\n        env_args = {\n            \"bddl_file_name\": task_bddl,\n            \"camera_heights\": 256,\n            \"camera_widths\": 256,\n            \"has_renderer\": False,\n            \"has_offscreen_renderer\": True,\n            \"use_camera_obs\": True,\n            \"camera_names\": [\"agentview\", \"robot0_eye_in_hand\"],\n        }\n        env = OffScreenRenderEnv(**env_args)\n\n        task_successes = 0\n        for trial in range(args.n_rollouts):\n            set_seed(args.seed + trial)\n            init_state = init_states[trial % len(init_states)]\n\n            success, steps = run_rollout(\n                env, init_state, model, device, task_desc, max_steps,\n                norm_stats, unnorm_key, preprocessor, args.sequence_length,\n            )\n            task_successes += int(success)\n            total_success += int(success)\n            total_episodes += 1\n\n            status = \"OK\" if success else \"FAIL\"\n            print(f\"  task {task_id}/{num_tasks} [{task_name}] \"\n                  f\"trial {trial+1}/{args.n_rollouts}: {status} ({steps} steps)\")\n\n        task_sr = task_successes / args.n_rollouts\n        results[task_name] = {\"success_rate\": task_sr, \"successes\": task_successes, \"trials\": args.n_rollouts}\n        print(f\"[a1-eval] Task {task_id} [{task_name}]: SR={task_sr:.1%} ({task_successes}/{args.n_rollouts})\")\n\n        env.close()\n\n    overall_sr = total_success / total_episodes if total_episodes > 0 else 0.0\n    print(f\"\\n[a1-eval] Overall: SR={overall_sr:.1%} ({total_success}/{total_episodes})\")\n\n    summary = {\n        \"model\": \"a1-libero\",\n        \"benchmark\": args.benchmark,\n        \"mode\": \"vla_only\",\n        \"seed\": args.seed,\n        \"n_rollouts\": args.n_rollouts,\n        \"overall_success_rate\": overall_sr,\n        \"total_successes\": total_success,\n        \"total_episodes\": total_episodes,\n        \"per_task\": results,\n    }\n\n    out_path = Path(args.output_file)\n    out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    out_path.write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=2))\n    print(f\"[a1-eval] Results saved to {args.output_file}\")\n    return summary\n\n\ndef set_seed(args.seed + trial)\n298\t            init_state = init_states[trial % len(init_states)]\n299\t\n300\t            success, steps = run_rollout(\n301\t                env, init_state, model, device, task_desc, max_steps,\n302\t                norm_stats, unnorm_key, preprocessor, args.sequence_length,\n303\t            )\n304\t            task_successes += int(success)\n305\t [{task_name}] \"\n310\t                  f\"trial {trial+1}/{args.n_rollouts}: {status} ({steps} steps)\")\n311\t\n312\t        task_sr = task_successes / args.n_rollouts\n313\t ({task_successes}/{args.n_rollouts})\")\n315\t\n316\t        env.close()\n317\t\n318\t    overall_sr = total_success / total_episodes if total_episodes > 0 else 0.0\n319\t    print(f\"\\n[a1-eval] return np.where(mask, 0.5 * (normalized + 1) * (q99 - q01) + q01, normalized)\n\n\ndef get_libero_image(obs):\n    return obs[\"agentview_image\"][::-1]\n\n\ndef get_libero_wrist_image(obs):\n    return obs[\"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\"][::-1]\n\n\ndef prepare_obs(obs, resize_size):\n    img = get_libero_image(obs)\n    wrist = get_libero_wrist_image(obs)\n    img_pil = Image.fromarray(img).resize(resize_size, Image.BILINEAR)\n    wrist_pil = Image.fromarray(wrist).resize(resize_size, Image.BILINEAR)\n    state = np.concatenate([\n        obs[\"robot0_eef_pos\"],\n        quat2axisangle(obs[\"robot0_eef_quat\"]),\n        obs[\"robot0_gripper_qpos\"],\n    ])\n    return np.array(img_pil), np.array(wrist_pil), state\n\n\n@torch.no_grad()\ndef predict_actions(model, device, img_np, wrist_np, state, task_desc,\n                    norm_stats, unnorm_key, preprocessor, sequence_length):\n    proprio = torch.tensor(state, dtype=torch.float32).unsqueeze(0)\n    gripper_raw = proprio[..., -1]\n    gripper_norm = torch.clamp(2.0 * (gripper_raw - 0.02) / (0.04 + 1e-8) - 1.0, -1.0, 1.0)\n\n    proprio_np = proprio[..., :-1].numpy()\n    proprio_stats = norm_stats[unnorm_key][\"proprio\"]\n    proprio_np = normalize_proprio_bounds_q99(proprio_np, proprio_stats)\n    proprio[..., :-1] = torch.from_numpy(proprio_np).float()\n    proprio[..., -1] = gripper_norm\n\n    proprio = proprio.unsqueeze(1).numpy()\n    pad_width = [(0, 0)] * (proprio.ndim - 1) + [(0, model.config.proprio_dim - proprio.shape[-1])]\n    proprio = np.pad(proprio, pad_width, constant_values=0)\n\n    dummy_action = np.zeros((model.config.num_actions_chunk, model.config.action_dim), dtype=np.float32)\n    input_data = {\n        \"question\": task_desc,\n        \"proprio\": proprio,\n        \"action\": dummy_action,\n        \"action_pad_mask\": np.zeros_like(dummy_action, dtype=bool),\n        \"answer\": \"Action\",\n        \"style\": \"action\",\n        \"metadata\": {},\n        \"images\": [Image.fromarray(img_np), Image.fromarray(wrist_np)],\n    }\n\n    processed = preprocessor(input_data)\n\n    collator = MMCollatorForAction(\n        model_config=model.config,\n        use_proprio=True,\n        max_sequence_length=sequence_length,\n        include_metadata=False,\n        pad=\"to_max\",\n        max_crops=model.config.get_max_crops(),\n    )\n    batch = collator([processed])\n    for k in batch:\n        if isinstance(batch[k], torch.Tensor):\n            batch[k] = batch[k].to(device)\n\n    model_inputs = {\n        \"input_ids\": batch[\"input_ids\"],\n        \"images\": batch.get(\"images\"),\n        \"image_masks\": batch.get(\"image_masks\"),\n        \"attention_mask\": batch.get(\"attention_mask\"),\n        \"attention_bias\": batch.get(\"attention_bias\"),\n        \"response_mask\": (batch[\"loss_masks\"] > 0) if \"loss_masks\" in batch else None,\n        \"image_input_idx\": batch.get(\"image_input_idx\"),\n        \"subsegment_ids\": batch.get(\"subsegment_ids\"),\n        \"position_ids\": batch.get(\"position_ids\"),\n        \"action_proprio\": batch.get(\"proprio\"),\n        \"proprio_token_idx\": batch.get(\"proprio_token_idx\"),\n        \"output_hidden_states\": False,\n        \"use_cache\": True,\n    }\n\n    norm_actions = model.predict_actions(**model_inputs)\n    norm_actions = norm_actions.to(torch.float32).cpu().numpy()\n    norm_actions = norm_actions[..., :7]\n\n    action_stats = norm_stats[unnorm_key][\"action\"]\n    actions = unnormalize_actions_bounds_q99(norm_actions, action_stats)\n\n    if actions.ndim == 3:\n        actions = actions[0]\n    return actions\n\n\ndef process_action(action):\n    a = action.copy()\n    a[-1] = 1.0 if a[-1] > 0.5 else -1.0\n    a[-1] = -a[-1]\n    return a\n\n\ndef run_rollout(env, init_state, model, device, task_desc, max_steps,\n                norm_stats, unnorm_key, preprocessor, sequence_length):\n    env.reset()\n    obs = env.set_init_state(init_state)\n\n    for _ in range(NUM_STEPS_WAIT):\n        obs, _, _, _ = env.step(np.array([0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, -1], dtype=np.float64))\n\n    resize_size = tuple(model.config.vision_backbone.image_default_input_size)\n    action_queue = deque()\n\n    for step in range(max_steps):\n        try:\n            if len(action_queue) == 0:\n                img_np, wrist_np, state = prepare_obs(obs, resize_size)\n                chunk = predict_actions(\n                    model, device, img_np, wrist_np, state, task_desc,\n                    norm_stats, unnorm_key, preprocessor, sequence_length,\n                )\n                for i in range(min(NUM_OPEN_LOOP_STEPS, len(chunk))):\n                    action_queue.append(chunk[i])\n\n            raw_action = action_queue.popleft()\n            action = process_action(raw_action)\n            obs, reward, done, info = env.step(action.tolist())\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"[rollout] step {step}: {e}\")\n            traceback.print_exc()\n            return False, step\n\n        if done or info.get(\"success\", False):\n            return True, step + 1\n\n    return False, max_steps\n\n\ndef evaluate(args):\n    set_seed(args.seed)\n    device = torch.device(f\"cuda:{args.gpu}\")\n\n    print(f\"[a1-eval] benchmark={args.benchmark}, n_rollouts={args.n_rollouts}, seed={args.seed}\")\n\n    model = load_a1_model(args.checkpoint, device, tokenizer_path=getattr(args, 'tokenizer_path', None))\n    norm_stats = load_norm_stats(args.checkpoint)\n    unnorm_key = UNNORM_KEY_MAP[args.benchmark]\n    assert unnorm_key in norm_stats, f\"Key {unnorm_key} not in norm_stats\"\n\n    preprocessor = build_mm_preprocessor(\n        model_config=model.config,\n        shuffle_messages=True,\n        require_image_features=True,\n        is_training=False,\n    )\n\n    max_steps = SUITE_MAX_STEPS[args.benchmark]\n    libero_suite_name = SUITE_TO_LIBERO[args.benchmark]\n\n    task_suite = benchmark.get_benchmark(libero_suite_name)()\n    num_tasks = task_suite.n_tasks\n\n    print(f\"[a1-eval] suite={libero_suite_name}, tasks={num_tasks}, max_steps={max_steps}\")\n\n    from libero.libero.envs import OffScreenRenderEnv\n    results = {}\n    total_success = 0\n    total_episodes = 0\n\n    for task_id in range(num_tasks):\n        task = task_suite.get_task(task_id)\n        task_name = task.name\n        task_desc = task.language\n        task_bddl = task_suite.get_task_bddl_file_path(task_id)\n        init_states = task_suite.get_task_init_states(task_id)\n\n        env_args = {\n            \"bddl_file_name\": task_bddl,\n            \"camera_heights\": 256,\n            \"camera_widths\": 256,\n            \"camera_names\": [\"agentview\", \"robot0_eye_in_hand\"],\n            \"render_gpu_device_id\": int(os.environ.get(\"MUJOCO_EGL_DEVICE_ID\", \"0\")),\n        }\n        env = OffScreenRenderEnv(**env_args)\n\n        task_successes = 0\n        for trial in range(args.n_rollouts):\n            set_seed(args.seed + trial)\n            init_state = init_states[trial % len(init_states)]\n\n            success, steps = run_rollout(\n                env, init_state, model, device, task_desc, max_steps,\n                norm_stats, unnorm_key, preprocessor, args.sequence_length,\n            )\n            task_successes += int(success)\n            total_success += int(success)\n            total_episodes += 1\n\n            status = \"OK\" if success else \"FAIL\"\n            print(f\"  task {task_id}/{num_tasks} [{task_name}] \"\n                  f\"trial {trial+1}/{args.n_rollouts}: {status} ({steps} steps)\")\n\n        task_sr = task_successes / args.n_rollouts\n        results[task_name] = {\"success_rate\": task_sr, \"successes\": task_successes, \"trials\": args.n_rollouts}\n        print(f\"[a1-eval] Task {task_id} [{task_name}]: SR={task_sr:.1%} ({task_successes}/{args.n_rollouts})\")\n\n        env.close()\n\n    overall_sr = total_success / total_episodes if total_episodes > 0 else 0.0\n    print(f\"\\n[a1-eval] Overall: SR={overall_sr:.1%} ({total_success}/{total_episodes})\")\n\n    summary = {\n        \"model\": \"a1-libero\",\n        \"benchmark\": args.benchmark,\n        \"mode\": \"vla_only\",\n        \"seed\": args.seed,\n        \"n_rollouts\": args.n_rollouts,\n        \"overall_success_rate\": overall_sr,\n        \"total_successes\": total_success,\n        \"total_episodes\": total_episodes,\n        \"per_task\": results,\n    }\n\n    out_path = Path(args.output_file)\n    out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    out_path.write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=2))\n    print(f\"[a1-eval] Results saved to {args.output_file}\")\n    return summary\n\n\ndef main():\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    p.add_argument(\"--benchmark\", default=\"object\", choices=[\"object\", \"spatial\", \"goal\", \"long\", \"90\"])\n    p.add_argument(\"--checkpoint\", default=\"/root/autodl-tmp/models/a1-libero/\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--n_rollouts\", type=int, default=20)\n    p.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    p.add_argument(\"--gpu\", type=int, default=0)\n    p.add_argument(\"--sequence_length\", type=int, default=1024)\n    p.add_argument(\"--output_file\", default=None)\n    p.add_argument(\"--tokenizer_path\", default=None)\n    args = p.parse_args()\n\n    if args.output_file is None:\n        args.output_file = f\"logs/eval_{args.benchmark}_a1_vla_only_seed{args.seed}.json\"\n\n    evaluate(args)\n\n\nif __name__ == {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_016fKQTWs6qbSDNPk2hSVCFT","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"Good news: the env created successfully and rollouts started. But there's a shape mismatch in proprio normalization.\n\nThe error: `(1,7) (8,)` — proprio has shape `(1,7)` (7D: eef_pos(3) + axisangle(3) + gripper_qpos(1)), but the norm stats (q01/q99) have shape `(8,)`.\n\nLooking at the code:\n```python\nproprio = torch.tensor(state, dtype=torch.float32).unsqueeze(0)  # (1, 8) if state is 8D\n\n\n```\n\nI'm extracting the gripper value and removing it from proprio to get the 7D vector, but the issue is that `state` from `prepare_obs` is actually concatenating the gripper as 2 values, not 1 — it's combining eef_pos (3D), axis-angle rotation (3D), and gripper_qpos (2D) for a total of 8D, which is why the normalization stats expect 8 dimensions. I need to check how A1's original code handles the gripper proprioception in LIBERO, since the robot returns 2 finger positions but the normalization stats might only expect 1
